2. These bail applications concern 98 crimes registered in
various police stations alleging offences punishable under
Sections 406, 409 and 420 r/w 34 of the Indian Penal Code,
Section 5 of the Kerala Protection of Interests of Depositors in
Financial Establishment Act, 2013 and Sections 3 r/w 21 (2)(3),
5 r/w 23 of the Buds Act, 2019. The petitioner is stated to be a
director in the four companies named Fashion Gold International
Pvt.Ltd, Nujoom Gold Pvt.Ltd, Qamar Fashion Gold Pvt.Ltd and
Fashion Ornaments Pvt. Ltd.

B.A.No.10317/2023 & Conn.Cases
108
3. The common allegation is that on 01.02.2019, accused
No.1 who is the Managing Director and accused No. 2 who is the
Chairman of Fashion Gold International Private Limited,
Cheruvathur, Kasargod induced the defacto complainant to
deposit in their business by assuring appropriate profit and to
return the amount whenever she asked for the same, and later
refused to re-deliver invested amount and failed to pay the
dividend or profit from November 2019 onwards and thereby
committed the offences as alleged.
4. The learned counsel appearing for the petitioner and the
learned Public Prosecutor were heard.
5. Earlier, this Court had already considered a batch of identical
cases in B.A. No.5273/2023 and connected cases dated
05.12.2023 and granted anticipatory bail to the petitioners
therein. On the grounds of parity, the petitioner herein is also
entitled to get similar treatment. In such circumstances, I am
inclined to hold that the custodial interrogation of the petitioner
is not necessary. Accordingly, anticipatory bail is granted,
ensuring the cooperation of the petitioner.
 The petitioner, in these bail applications are directed to

B.A.No.10317/2023 & Conn.Cases
109
surrender before the Investigating Officer within three weeks
from today for interrogation. After interrogation, he shall be
released on bail on executing a bond for Rs.50,000 (Rupees Fifty
Thousand only)with two solvent sureties each for the like sum to
the satisfaction of the Investigating Officer on the following
conditions:
(i) The petitioner shall appear as and when
directed by the Investigating Officer, cooperate
with the Investigation and shall make available all
the documents/information sought for by the
Investigating Officer.
(ii) The petitioner shall not intimidate or attempt
to influence the witnesses, tamper with any
evidence, or get involved in similar offences while
on bail.
(iii) The Directors (accused Nos.5 to 9, – Sri. M.C.
Kamarudeen, Sri.T, K Pookkoya Thangal, Sri.
Haris Abdul Khadar, Sri. Sainul Abideen, Sri.
Hisham Ancharappattil-) shall surrender their
passports, if any. If they have no passports, they

B.A.No.10317/2023 & Conn.Cases
110
shall file affidavits to that effect before the court
concerned on the date of execution of the bond or
within two weeks from today.
(iv) The petitioner shall furnish their address and
their phone numbers to the Court as well as to
the Investigating Officer;
(v) The petitioner shall not leave India without
previous permission of the jurisdictional court.
(vi) In case of violation of any of the above
conditions, the jurisdictional Court shall be
empowered to consider the application for
cancellation, if any, and pass appropriate orders
in accordance with law, notwithstanding the bail
having been granted by this Court.
It is made clear that it is within the power of police to
investigate the matter and if necessary to effect recoveries on
the information if any given by the petitioner, even when the
petitioner is on bail as per the judgment of the Apex Court in
Sushila Aggarwal and others V. State ( NCT of Delhi) and
another [2021 (1) KHC 663].

B.A.No.10317/2023 & Conn.Cases
111
These bail applications are allowed as above.
 Sd/-
 MOHAMMED NIAS C.P.,
 JUDGE
mtk
